1b1(mdo sna tshogs): @#//rgya gar skad du/bu khyu ba la a dA na/bod skad du/bsod nams 
1b2(mdo sna tshogs): stobs kyi rtogs pa brjod pa/bam po dang po//sangs rgyas la phyag 'tshal lo//'di skad 
1b3(mdo sna tshogs): bdag gis thos pa dus gcig na/bcom ldan 'das mnyan du yod pa na/rgyal bu rgyal byed kyi 
1b4(mdo sna tshogs): tshal mgon med zas sbyin gyi kun dga' rab na bzhugs te/de nas bcom ldan 'das kyis dge 
1b5(mdo sna tshogs): slong rnams la bka' stsal pa/dge slong dag 'di na mi blun po la la dam pa'i chos ma yin pa 
1b6(mdo sna tshogs): gsum dang ldan na sbyin pa dag mi sbyin zhing bsod nams dag mi byed la/tshul khrims yang dag 
1b7(mdo sna tshogs): par blangs nas gnas par mi byed de/gsum po gang zhe na/'di ltar chags pa dang ser sna dang 
1b8(mdo sna tshogs): bag med pa rnams te/dge slong dag 'di na ma blun po la la dam pa'i tshes ma yin pa gsum 
2a1(mdo sna tshogs): po 'di dang ldan pas sbyin pa dag ma byin zhing bsod nams dag ma byas la/tshul khrims yang dag par blangs te gnas par ma byas pas lus zhig ste shi ba'i 'og tu ngan 'gro log 
2a2(mdo sna tshogs): par ltung ba sems can dmyal ba rnams su skye bar 'gyur ro//dge slong dag skyes bu dam pa chos gsum dang ldan pa ni sbyin pa dag sbyin zhing bsod nams dag byed la tshul khrims yang dag par 
2a3(mdo sna tshogs): blangs te gnas so//gsum gang zhe na/'di ltar ma chags pa dang /ser sna'i dri ma dang bral ba dang /bag yod pa rnams so//dge slong dag chos gsum po 'di dag dang ldan na skyes bu dam pa sbyin pa 
2a4(mdo sna tshogs): dag sbyin zhing bsod nams kyi dag byas la/tshul khrims yang bdag par blangs te gnas bas lus zhig ste shi ba'i 'ogs tu bde 'gro mtho ris kyi 'jig rten du lha rnams kyi nang du skya bar 'gyur ro//
2a5(mdo sna tshogs): bcom ldan 'das kyis de skad ces bka' stsal te/bde bar gshegs pas de skad ces gsungs nas/de'i 'og tu gzhan yang ston pas 'di skad ces gsungs so//gang phyir ser sna bag med 
2a6(mdo sna tshogs): pas/sbyin pa sbyin par mi byed do//de phyir bsod nams 'dod pa yi//shes pas sbyin pa sbyin par bya//ser sna can gyi 'jigs pa ni//bkres dang skom pa 'di yin na//gang gis 'jigs pas 'dir ma byin//
2a7(mdo sna tshogs): de nyid ma yin 'jigs pa ste//'jig rten 'di dang pha rol tu//de yi byis pa gos par 'gyur//gang dag ser sna sbangs nas kyang //dus su sbyin pa sbyin byed na//shi yang de ni shi ma yin//brgyags bzang ba yi 
2a8(mdo sna tshogs): 'dron po bzhin//gang dag shin tu gtang dka' ba//nyung ngu'am mang po gtong byed na//gtang dka' gtong ba de dag ni//dka' ba'i las dag byed pa yin//dam pa min pas mi rtogs te//dam pa chos las 
2b1(mdo sna tshogs): 'da' mi byed//de phyir dam pa dam pa byin//'di nas shi bar gyur pa na//dam pa ma yin dmyal bar 'gro//dam pa dag ni mtho ris 'gro//rga bas gtses pa la ni skyabs med de//tshe thung bas na srog khyer dus kyang nye//
2b2(mdo sna tshogs): 'chi bas 'jigs pa 'di mthong rnams kyis ni//bsod nams bde sbyor bdag ni bya ba'i rigs//bud med spangs 'dir bor bas 'tsho ba gang //dga' bas sbyin pa nyung ngu byin na yang //stong gi mchod sbyin brgya stong 
2b3(mdo sna tshogs): byed pa ni//de yi bcu drug char yang mi phod do//ci phyir mchod sbyin yangs shing rgya che ba//mchod sbyin 'thun pa de yi lam mi 'gro//ji ltar stong gi mchod sbyin stong rnams kyang //de yi bcu drug char mi 'gyur zhe na//
2b4(mdo sna tshogs): byis pa la la gsod cing 'ching byed 'dir//gzhan nor sbyin byed ya dar brlag par 'gyur//mchi ma gdong bcas chad par bcas pa yi//yon de mchod sbyin 'thun pa'i lam mi 'gro//de ltar mchod sbyin yangs 
2b5(mdo sna tshogs): shing rgya che ba//mchod sbyina'athun pa'i lam du mi 'gro la//de ltar stong gi mchod sbyin stod rnams kyang //de yi bcug char yang mi phod do////la la gang dag gzu lums ma yin pa//chos bzhin nor rnams thob pa gtong 
2b6(mdo sna tshogs): byed cing //de dag gtang dka' de rnams btang bas na//rang gi bsod nams kyis ni lha gnas 'gro//bcom ldan 'das kyis de skad ces bka' stsal nas/bcom ldan 'das gdan las bzhengs te/nags stug po 
2b7(mdo sna tshogs): ga la ba de logs su gshegs so//gshegs nas nags stug po'i nang du zhugs te/nyin par gnas pa'i phyir shing zhig gi drung na gnas shing bzhugs so//de'i tshe dge slong mang po dag 'dun pa 'du nus shing 
2b8(mdo sna tshogs): bakhod pa'i skabs su tshul 'di lta bur tshe dang ldan pa dag mi rnams kyi nyams su dben pa gang yin zhes gtam du glengs so//de'i tshe bcom ldan 'das kyi gcung sru'i bu tshe dang ldan pa dga' bo dang /tshe dang ldan pa gro zhin 
3a1(mdo sna tshogs): skyes bye ba nyi shu pa dang /tshe dang ldan pa ma 'gags pa dang /tshe dang ldan par sha ri'i bu rnams kyang 'khor de nyid du 'dus par gyur te 'khod do//de nas tshe dang ldan pa dga' bos smras pa/tshe dang ldan 
3a2(mdo sna tshogs): pa dag byang gzugs ni mi rnams kyi nyams su dben pa yin no//tshe dang ldan pa gro zhin skyes bye ba nyi shu pas smras pa/tshe dang ldan pa dag brtson 'grus ni mi rnams kyi nyams su/dben pa yin no//tshe dad ldan pa ma 'gags 
3a3(mdo sna tshogs): pas smras pa tshe dang ldan pa dag bzod pa ni mi rnams kyi nyams su bden pa yin no//tshe dang dang ldan pa shA ri'i bus smras pa/tshe dang ldan pa'i dag shes rab 'ba' shig mi rnams kyi nyams su dang /de dag 'di 
3a4(mdo sna tshogs): skad du/tshe dang ldan pa dag bdag cag kun mi mthun par gyur te/'di ltar tha dad par brtags kyis/bdag cag bcom ldan 'das kyi thad du song ste don 'di nyid zhus la/bcom ldan 'das bdag cag la ci skad du lung 
3a5(mdo sna tshogs): ston pa de bzhin du gzung bar bya'o zhes zer nas tshe dang ldan pa dag bcom ldan 'das ni/ston pa thugs rje chen po ste//thams cad mkhyen pa the tshom bsal//de ni som nyi gcod pa ste//nyi mas mun pa bsal ba bzhin//sdug bsngal dra 
3a6(mdo sna tshogs): bas glags pa dang //log pa'i lam la gnas pa yi//srog chags rnams kyi skyabs gcig ste//bu rnams kyi ni pha mchog bzhin//de phyir chos kun dbang phyug po//sems can kun gyi don brtson pa//thub pa the tshom gcod de 
3a7(mdo sna tshogs): la//bdag cag kun gyis zhu bar bya//som nyi mdud pa chen po ni//gang phyir rgyal ba gcod par 'gyur//de phyir thams cad dong nas ni//de bzhin gshegs la zhu bar bya//zhes byas nas dge slong mang po de dag bcom ldan 
3a8(mdo sna tshogs): 'das blta ba'i phyir 'gro bar chas pa las/bcom ldan 'das nyin par gnas pa'i phyir nags stug po'i nang na bzhugs pa'i bar skabs su dge slong mang po dge 'dun khang du 'dus shing 'khod de/skabs su tshul de lta 
3b1(mdo sna tshogs): bu'i gtam glengs pa/lha'i snyan rnam par dag pa mi las 'das pas gsan to//gsan nas ting nge 'dzin de las bzhengs te 'dun khang gal 'dug par gshegs so//gshegs nas dge slong gi dge 
3b2(mdo sna tshogs): 'dun gyi gud la gdan bshams pa de nyid la bzhugs so//bzhugs nas bcom ldan 'das kyis dge slong rnams la bka' stsal pa dang /dge slong dag dge slong mang po 'dun khang du 'dus shing 'khod pa rnams 
3b3(mdo sna tshogs): kyis skabs kyi gtam ci zhig glengs da ltar khyed gtam ji lta bu zhig gi phyir 'dus shing 'khod/gsol pa/btsun pa bdag cag dge slong mang po 'dun pa 'dir 'dus shing mchis pa rnams kyis skabs 
3b4(mdo sna tshogs): su tshul 'di lta bur tshe dang ldan pa dag mi rnams kyi nyams su dben pa gang lags shes bka' mchid bgyis so//de la tshe dang ldan pa dga' bo'i mchid nas ni tshe dang ldan pa dag byang gzugs mi rnams kyi 
3b5(mdo sna tshogs): nyams su dben pa lags so zhes mchi/tshe dang ldan pa gro zhin skyes bye ba nyi shu ba'i mchid nas ni tshe dang dang ldan pa dag brtson 'grus mi rnams kyi nyams su dbain pa lags so zhes mchi//tshe dang ldan pa ma 'gags 
3b6(mdo sna tshogs): pa'i mchid nas ni tshe dang /ldan pa dag bzo mi rnams kyi nyams su dpen pa lags so zhes mchi/cho dang ldan pa shA ri'i bu'i mchid nas ni tshe dang lda pa dag shes rab 'ba' shig mi rnams kyi nyams su dpen pa lags 
3b7(mdo sna tshogs): so zhes mchi ste/btsun pa bdag cag rnams 'di snyam bgyid do//tshe dang ldan pa dag bdag cag kun mi mthun par 'gyur te/'di ltar tha dad par brtags kyis bdag cag bcom ldan 'das kyi thad du mchis te 
3b8(mdo sna tshogs): don 'di nyid zhus la/bcom ldan 'das bdag cag la ji skad du lung ston pa de bzhin gzung bar bgyi'o snyam bgyid do//btsun pa bdag cag dge slong mang po 'dun khang du 'dus shing mchis pa rnams kyis 
4a1(mdo sna tshogs): @#//skabs kyi bka' mchid 'di glengs te/de ltar bdag cag bka' mchid 'di'i slad du 'dus shing mches lags so//de nas bcom ldan 'das kyis de'i tshe tshigs su bcad de gsungs ba/sems 
4a2(mdo sna tshogs): can rnams kyi gzugs dang bzo//brtson 'grus dang ni shes rab gzhan//bsod nams dag las 'byung bas na//de phyir bsod nams dam pa yin//zhes gsungs nas dge slong rnams la bka' stsal pa/
4a3(mdo sna tshogs): dge slong dag ci yang rung ste/lan 'ga' tsam du zad kyi dus thams cad du kun nyams su deban pa ma yin no//bsod nams ni dus thams cad du srog chags thams cad kyi nyams su dben pa yin te/dge slong dag 
4a4(mdo sna tshogs): 'di ltar bsod nams bzhin du sems can rnams kyi nyams su dben pa'i chos gcig kyang ngas yang dag par rjes su ma mthong ngo//dge slong dag de ci'i phyir zhe na/sngon byung ba 'das pa'i dus na/rgyal po'i pho brang 
4a5(mdo sna tshogs): 'khor zhig na/rgyal po gsal stobs zhes bya ba zhig rgyal po byed de de'i ring la 'byor ba dang /rgyas pa dang /bde ba dang /lo legs pa dang /skye bo mang po dang /mi mang pos gang ngo//rgyal po de'i btsun mo rnam par 
4a6(mdo sna tshogs): spa ba zhes bya ba gzugs bzang shing blta na sdug pa mdzes pa zhig yod de/phyi zhig na btsun mo rnam par spa ba dang lhan cig tu rtse zhing dga' la dga' mgur spyod do//de rtse zhing dga' la dga' 'gur spyod pa de las 
4a7(mdo sna tshogs): bu gzugs bzang zhing bltan sdug la mdzes pa/si'i kha dog las ni 'das/lha'i kha dog ni ma thob pa zhig btsas te/de btsas pa na btsas ston byas so//btsas ston byas nas gzugs stobs shes bya bar ming 
4a8(mdo sna tshogs): btags so//de bzhin du rim gyis de'i 'og tu de'i bu gsum btsas te/de dag gi nang na gcig ni brtson 'grus dang ldan pa zhes bya/gnyis pa ni bzo ldan zhes bya/gsum pa ni shes rab dang ldan pa zhes bya'o//phyi re 
4b1(mdo sna tshogs): re shig na yang btsun mo rnam par spa ba la bu chags te/gang gi nyin par de'i bu chags pa de nyid kyi nyin par glo bur du pho brang 'khor tu gser rnam pa sna tshogs kyi char bab po//btsun mo de'i steng du yang lha'i bla re chen po 
4b2(mdo sna tshogs): rin po che sna tshogs kyis spras pa zhig byung ngo//de nas rgyal po gsal stobs kyis ya mtshan de mthong nas shin du ngo mtshar du gyur te/ltas pa rnams la smras pa/kye sngon ma byung ba'i ya mtshan byung ba 'di ci zhig/ltas pa 
4b3(mdo sna tshogs): rnams kyis gsol la/lha btsun mo'i lhums su sras zhugs pa gang lags pa 'di ni bsod nams kyis che bar grags pa zhig ste/sems can de'i mthu lags so//de nas rgyal pos de thos nas shin tu ngo 
4b4(mdo sna tshogs): mtshar tu gyur te/de nas re shig na btsun mo de 'di lta bur yid la 'dod pa skyes te/kye ma bdag seng ge'i khri la 'dug cing steng du gdugs dkar po thogs la rnga yab nor bu'i yu ba can yang thogs na ci ma rung 
4b5(mdo sna tshogs): snyam ste/des rgyal po la gsol to//de nas rgyal po sems rab tu dga' bar gyur nas/grong khyer rgya cher mdzes par byed du bcug nas de bzhin du thams cad byed du bcug ste/de'i yid la 'dod pa skyes pa gang 
4b6(mdo sna tshogs): yin pa de med par gyur to//de nas yang de'i yid la kye ma bdag gser dngul gyi phung po'i steng du 'dug la rang gi lag gis sbyin pa rnams sbyin zhing bsod nams dag byed de/nor med pa rnams nor dang 
4b7(mdo sna tshogs): ldan par byed na ci ma rung snyam du 'dod pa skyes so//des rgyal po la gsol to/de nas rgyal pos de bzhin du byed du bcug nas de'i yid la 'dod pa skyes ba gang yin pa de med par gyur te/de nas yang de'i 
4b8(mdo sna tshogs): yid la kye ma btson thams cad btang na ci ma rung snyam du 'dong pa skyes so//de nas rgyal pos de'i yid la 'dod pa skye ba bstsal ba'i phyir btson thams cad gtong du bcug ste/de'i yid 'dod pa skyes pa 
5a1(mdo sna tshogs): @#//gang yin pa de med par gyur to//de nas yang de'i yid la kye ma bdag bskyed mos tshal dag mthong na ci ma rung snyam du 'dod pa skyes so//de nas de rgyal pos bskyed mos tshal mdzes pa dag ltar 
5a2(mdo sna tshogs): bcug ste/de'i yid la 'dod pa skyes pa gang yin pa de med par 'gyur to//de nas yang de yid la kye ma bdag gis skye bo mang po'i mdun du tshul 'di lta bur bden pa'i tshig brjod de/ji ste bsod nams dag gi 
5a3(mdo sna tshogs): rnam par smin pa yod ce na/de las lha'i seng ge'i khri gang la 'dug na/skye bo mang po la chos ston cing bdag gi gtam gyi rjes la yang ya mtshan 'ga' 'byung bar 'gyur ba de lta bu lha rnams kyis myur du bdag la 
5a4(mdo sna tshogs): phul na ci ma rung snyam du 'dong pa skyes te/des rgyal po la gsol to//de nas rgyal pos btsun mo 'khor dang grong khyer rgya cher mdzes par byed du bcug go/grong khyer na gnas pa'i skye bo mang po thams cad kyang gos 
5a5(mdo sna tshogs): gtsang ma dang rgyan bgos nas/spos dang /phreng ba dang /byug pa lag na thogs te 'dus par gyur to//de nas btsun mo rnam par spa ba rgyan thams cad kyis blubs te/bud med kyi 'khor kyi dkyil 'khor gyis bskor 
5a6(mdo sna tshogs): nas lha'i bu mo bzhin du skye bo dag gi mig dang /yid 'phrog cing skye bo mang po de dag gi nang du song ste/sems can thams cad kyi rjes su 'gro ba'i byams pa'i sems nye bar bzhag nas nam mkha' la lta zhing bden 
5a7(mdo sna tshogs): pa'i byin gyi rlabs byed de tshigs su bcad pa smras pa/mi dbang lha yi rnam thar 'grub pa yi//bsod nams stobs 'phags ji ste bdag la yod//bden pa des na deng du lha rnams kyis//myur du bdag gi seng 
5a8(mdo sna tshogs): ge'i khri shoms shig/de nas tshig de skad smras ma thag tu bsod nams dag gi mthu ston pa'i lha rnams kyis glo bur du seng ge'i khri phul te/lha'i me tog gi char yang phab bo//de nas skye bo phal po che de dag gis 
5b1(mdo sna tshogs): lha dang mi mngon par dga' bar byang ba'i cho 'phrul ngo mtshar rmad du gyur pa de mthong nas shin du ya mtshan du gyur te/kye ma bsod nams dag gi mthu che ste/'di ltar 'jig rten thams cad kyi mchod pa'i gnas lha 
5b2(mdo sna tshogs): rnams kyang bsod nams kyi stobs kyis bkug ste/mi rnams kyi dag nyan pa ni ngo mtshar to zhes ched du rjod pa ched du brjod do//de nas btsun mo rnam par spa ba yid rab tu dga' ste/tsham tshom med par seng ge'i khri la 
5b3(mdo sna tshogs): 'dzegs nas 'dug go/btsun mo de 'dug ma thag tu sa rnam pa drug tu g.yos te/seng ge'i khri yang sa las mi bdun gyi srid tsam du nam mkha' la 'phags te 'dug go/de nas seng ge'i khri de'i steng du lha'i bla re 
5b4(mdo sna tshogs): rin po che sna tshogs kyis spras pa zhig byung ngo //de nas skye bo phal po che de dag bsod nams kyi mthu mthong bas sems dga' bar gyur te/spos dang /phreng ba dang /byug pa rnams kyis btsun mo la yang legs par 
5b5(mdo sna tshogs): mchod nas thal mo sbyar te/de'i dag nyan cing sems btud nas mdun du 'khod do//rgyal po gsal stobs kyang bsod nams kyi rlabs shin du rmad du byung ba de mthong nas sems kyi rgyud yang dag par 
5b6(mdo sna tshogs): dga' bar gyur te/'khor dang bcas par thal mo sbyar nas 'khod do//de nas btsun mo rnam par spa bas de'i tshe sngon ma byung ba'i tshigs su bcad pa 'di skad ces smras so//bsod nams bsags na 
5b7(mdo sna tshogs): bde bar 'gyur//de phyir mis ni bsod nams bya//de yang yang dang yang du bya//de nyid la ni 'dun par bya//tshigs su bcad pa 'di smras ma thag tu bar snang las/legs par smras so//
5b8(mdo sna tshogs): mchog tu legs par smras so zhes sgra byung ngo //lha'i rol mo'i sgra dag kyang sgrogs so//lha'i gos shin du spa ba'i char chen po yang bab bo//de nas tshigs su bcad pa 'di thos ma thag tu rgyal po 
6a1(mdo sna tshogs): @#//gsal stobs dang /skye bo phal po che de dag dga' bar gyur to//nam mkha' la 'khod pa'i lha rnams kyis kyang tshom tshom med par rang gi lus las bla gos dang rgyan rnams phud nas btsun mo 
6a2(mdo sna tshogs): rnam par sba ba dang /seng ge'i bri la bkab ste/tshig 'thun par legs par smras so//legs par smras so zhes sgra byung ngo //de nas btsun mo de seng ge'i khri de las dal gyis babs te sa la 'dug nas lha'i 
6a3(mdo sna tshogs): rol mo'i sgra rnams ni zhi bar gyur/mi rnams kyi rol mo'i sgra ni sgrogs so//de nas rgyal po de dang skye bo phal po che dga' bar gyur nas/bkur sti chen pos mchod cing btsun mo'i 'khor du bskyal te/btsun 
6a4(mdo sna tshogs): mo nang du song nas seng ge'i khri de mi snang bar gyur te/de nas skye bo phal po che dag gis bsod nams kyi rnam par g.yo ba de mthong ste/sems yang dag par dga' bar gyur nas/kye ma bsod nams dag gi tshe 
6a5(mdo sna tshogs): ba nyid/kye ma bsod nams dgag gi 'bras bu dang ra ba zhes ched du brjod pa ched du brjod do//de nas btsun mo de'i yid la 'dod pa skyes pa gang yin pa de med par gyur nas/phyis re shig na zla ba dgu tshad 
6a6(mdo sna tshogs): nas/btsun mo rnam par sba ba nyi ma shar ba'i dus kyi skabs su bu btsas te/khye'u gzugs bzang zhing blta na sdug la mdzes pa zhig bcas so//de'i skabs su yang sa rnam pa drug tu g.yos te/
6a7(mdo sna tshogs): nam mkha' las kyang rin po che sna bdun gyi char chen po btsun mo'i 'khor du bab po/rgyal po'i pho brang 'khor de dag thams cad du yang gos rnam pa sna tshogs kyi char bab po/rgyal po de'i yul thams 
6a8(mdo sna tshogs): cad du yang lha'i me tog mchog tu yid tu 'ong ba'i char bab po/me tog dang 'bras bu'i shing ljon pa thams cad las kyang me tog dang 'bras bu rnams 'thon to//char zim bu yang thams cad du bab pa 
6b1(mdo sna tshogs): phyogs bzhi nas kyang ser bu bsil ba langs so//de nas gzhon nu btsas ma thag pa phyogs gang na 'dug pa der rgyal po chen po bzhis lha'i seng ge'i khri rin po che sna tshogs kyis spras pas brtol te gla bur du 
6b2(mdo sna tshogs): phul lo//lha'i rgyal po brgya byin yang bdag nyid lag dar te/gzhon nu'i steng du lha'i gdugs rtsibs brgya ba dang /rnga yab nor bu'i yu ba can 'dzin to//de'i steng nas sum bcu rtsa gsum gyi lha rnams lha'i 
6b3(mdo sna tshogs): gos kyi bla re 'grems so//la la ni rin po che 'thor ro//la la ni rgyan 'thor ro//la la ni kos 'thor ro//la la ni me tog 'thor ro//la la ni phye ma 'thor ro//la la ni spos dang /phreng ba dang /byug 
6b4(mdo sna tshogs): pa rnams 'thor ro//la la ni lha'i rol mo'i sgra rnams legs par sgrogs so//lha'i bu las sna tshogs can gyis ni grong khyer de thams cad rdo dang /gseg ma dang /gye mo rnams med par byas te bzhag 
6b5(mdo sna tshogs): nas dar gyi lda ldi mang po ni btags/rgyal mtshan dang /ba dan ni bsgred/tsan dan gyi chus ni chag chag btab/bdug pa dri zhim po'i snod ni btags/me tog sna tshogs ni gtor te/lha rnams kyi 
6b6(mdo sna tshogs): bskyed mos tshal dga' ba'i tshal zhes bya ba dang 'dra bar yid du 'ong bar byas so//glang po che'i mchog brgya ni nags khung chen po nas rang 'ongs te glang po che'i bres na gnas par gyur te/rgod ma brgya 
6b7(mdo sna tshogs): yang rte'u 'brangs 'bru thams cad kyang ma rmos ma btab par mthu dang ldan par smin to//seng ge'i khri de'i 'og nas kyang gter chen po lnga rin po che sna tshogs kyis legs par yongs su gang ba rnams kyis 
6b8(mdo sna tshogs): kha bstan te/god par byas kyang yongs su zad par mi 'gyur ro//sems can phan tshun 'khod pa rnams kyang de'i yud tsam la byams pa'i sems su gyur to//gzhon du rdzu 'phrul che zhing mthu che ba de 
7a1(mdo sna tshogs): @#//btsas ma thag tu tshe rabs dran te/phyogs bzhir bltas nas tshigs su bcad de smras pa/bsod nams bsags nas bde bar 'gyur//de phyir mi yis bsod nams bya//de yang yang dang 
7a2(mdo sna tshogs): yang du bya//de nyid la ni 'dun par bya//zhes smras so//de nas nam mkha' la gnas pa'i lha rdzu 'phrul che zhing mthu che ba zhig gis bsod nams dag gi shin du rmad du byung ba shin du yid du 'ong ba rnam par 
7a3(mdo sna tshogs): g.yo ba de mthong nas sems yang dag par dga' bar gyur te/bsod nams dag gi che ba nyid bstan pa'i phyir de'i tshe tshigs su bcad de smras ba/bsod nams dag gi g.yo ba ltos//rgyal po chen po 
7a4(mdo sna tshogs): bzhi rnams dang //sum bcu rtsa gsum dbang por bcas//bsod nams kyis ni de'i dbang byas//shes smras so//de nas rgyal po de btsun mo'i 'khor dang /sras dang /blon po dang /pho brang 'khor gyi 
7a5(mdo sna tshogs): mi dang /yul gyi mi dang bcas pas phun sum tshogs pa de dag mthong nas ya mtshan gyis mig rgyas par gyur te/mchog tu ya mtshan du gyur nas/kye ma gzhon nu'i bsod nams kyi stobs kye ma gzhon nu'i 
7a6(mdo sna tshogs): bsod nams kyis che bar grags pa 'di ltar 'di mir gyur tu zin kyang 'di lta bur lha'i 'byor pa rgyas pa byung bar gyur pa 'di ni ya mtshan che'o zhes ched du rjod pa ched du brjod do//de nas rgyal po sems rab tu 
7a7(mdo sna tshogs): dga' zhing bu snying du sdug nas dkor ba rnams la bsgo ste/bka' snyan gsol ba rnams kyi phyir rgyal po'i ra bar gsar dngul gyi phung po spungs nas sbyin pa dag byin te bsod nams dag byas so//nor med pa du 
7a8(mdo sna tshogs): ma ni nor dang ldan pa la bkod do//de nas re shig 'byor pa chen po rgyas pas de btsas pa'i btsas ston byas nas ming 'dogs par byed de gzhon nu'i ming cir gdags shes zer ro//de nas blon po dag gis 
7b1(mdo sna tshogs): gsol pa/lha gang gi slad du sras 'di'i bsod nams kyi stobs rgyas shing /de btsas pa na 'di lta bu lha'i 'byor pa byung bar gyur pas na/de'i phyir sras 'di'i mtshan ni bsod nams stobs shes gdags pa'i 
7b2(mdo sna tshogs): rigs so//de nas de'i ming bsod nams stobs shes btags so//de nas gzhon nu bsod nams stobs bang na 'tsho ba'i ma ma gnyis dang nu ma snun pa'i ma ma gnyis dang /dri ma 'khru ba'i ma ma gnyis 
7b3(mdo sna tshogs): dang /rtse ba'i ma ma gnyis te ma ma brgyad la gtang nas/ma ma brgyad do de dag gis 'o ma dang /zho dang /mar dang /zhun mar dang /mar gyi snying khu dang /gzhan yang yo byad kyi khyad par bzang ngo cog gis bskyed 
7b4(mdo sna tshogs): bsrings te rdzing bu'i pad ma bzhin du skye drag par gyur to//gang gi tshe de cher skyes pa de'i tshe yi ge slob du bcug ste/des yi ge mthar phyin nas des rgyal po rgyal rigs dag gi gnas lnga rnams kyi bar gang 
7b5(mdo sna tshogs): yin pa de dag la byang bar gyur to//gzhon nu bsod nams stob de yang dad pa dang ldan la dge zhing bsam pa bzang ba/bdag la phan pa dang /gzhan la phan pa la zhugs pa/snying rje can/bdag 
7b6(mdo sna tshogs): nyid che ba/chos 'dod pa skye dgu la byams pa/gtong ba la sred pa/sbyin pa la dga' ba/rab tu sbyin pa la mngon par dga' ba thams cad sbyin pa/thams cad yongs su gtang ba/chags pa med par 
7b7(mdo sna tshogs): yongs su gtong ba/gtong ba chen po la gnas pa zhig ste/'di ltar dge sbyong dang /bram ze dang /bkren pa dang /sdug cing phongs pa rnams la tha na rang gi sha'i bar du bsam byin pa dang /yongs su ma btang ba ci 
7b8(mdo sna tshogs): yang med do//de la slong ba dag 'ongs te slong na de'i tshe de dag la byams pa'i sems kyis nye bar gnas te/nam mkha' la bltas nas kye ma 'di ltar gser ram/dngul lam/gos sam/bzar ba'am/
8a1(mdo sna tshogs): @#//btsa' ba 'am/btung ba 'am/mal stan nam/gzhan yang gang ci yang rung ste ci 'dod pa de dang de dag nam mkha' las bdag la 'ongs kyang ci ma rung snyam du sems bskyed de/ngas 
8a2(mdo sna tshogs): sems bskyed na de la 'ong bar 'gyur la su yang don de mi shes so//des yo byad de dag gis slong ba dag gi bsam pa yongs su rdzogs par byas nas phyir 'dod do//de'i grags pas 'dzam bu'i gling thams 
8a3(mdo sna tshogs): cad kun tu gang bar gyur te/'og tu klu'i 'jig rten thams cad nas steng du tshangs pa'i 'jig rten gyi bar du sgra grags so//de nas phyis re shig na gzhon nu bsod nams stobs phung po bzhi dang lhan gcig tu 
8a4(mdo sna tshogs): 'byor ba mchog gis bskyed mos tshal du 'khyam du dong ngo //de bar gyi lam du yi dgas mchu khab tsam kha lto ri rab tsam ste/keng rus bsgreng ba 'dra zhing lus shin tu 'apar ba thal mo sbyar ba stong phrag du ma dag 
8a5(mdo sna tshogs): gis yongs su bskor te/gzhon nu bsod nams stob ma gtogs par sus kyang yi dgas de dag mi mthong ngo //de nas yi dgas de dag gis gzhon nu bsod nams stongs la smras pa/gzhon nu khyod 
8a6(mdo sna tshogs): bsod nams kyis che bar grags pa/snying rje chen po yin na/bdag cag bkres shing skom pas nyams thag pa rnams la zas dang skom byin cig/bdag cag ni ser sna'i dbang du gyur te/yi dgas kyi 
8a7(mdo sna tshogs): 'jig rten du skyes pas lo brgya phrag du mar yang chu yang mi rnyed na kha zas lta ga la mthong de skad ces smras nas gzhon nu bsod nams s+hobs kyi sems snying rjes kun tu bskyod nas skyen par nam mkha' la 
8a8(mdo sna tshogs): bltas te/kye ma lha'i bza' ba dang btung ba bdag la 'ongs kyang ci ma rung snyam du sems kyang bskyed do//de nas blo bur du lha'i bza' ba dang btung pa nam mkha' las 'ongs so//de nas gzhon nu 
8b1(mdo sna tshogs): bsod nams stobs kyis yi dgas rnams lha'i bza' ba dang btung bas yang dag par tshim par bya ba la zhugs so//de nas re shig na yi dgas de dag gis sngon gyi las kyi nyes pas bza' ba dang /
8b2(mdo sna tshogs): btung ba de dag ma mthong nas 'di skad ces smras te/gzhon nu khyod snying rje can zhig ces grags na/ci'i phyir bdag cag tshim par mi byed/gzhon nus smras pa/lha'i bza' ba dang btung pa 'di dag byin pa yin 
8b3(mdo sna tshogs): nam/kha zas 'di dag las ci nyes te mi za/yi dgas rnams kyis smras pa/gzhon nu bdag cag ni sngon gyi las kyi nyes pas lha'i bza' ba dang btung? ba de dag mi mthong ngo //de nas gzhon nu bsod nams 
8b4(mdo sna tshogs): stobs mi dga' nas kye ma ser sna'i rnam par g.yo ba snyam du yid la bsams te/yi dgas de dag la byams pas khyab par byas nas sems bskyed de tshig tu smras pa/ji ste bsod nams dag gi mthu yod na 
8b5(mdo sna tshogs): bden pa de dang /bden pa'i tshig des yi dgas 'di dag gis lha'i bza' ba dang btung ba 'di dag yang dag pa ji lta ba bzhin du mthong zhing za ba'i mthu yod par gyur cig ces gzhon nus tshig tu brjod ma thag tu 
8b6(mdo sna tshogs): yi dgas rnams kyis lha'i bza' ba dang btud pa de dag log par mi mthong zhing de dag gi kha yang tha mal du gyur to//de nas gzhon nu ba sod nams stobs yid rab tu dga' bas yi dgas de dag lha'i 
8b7(mdo sna tshogs): bza' ba dang btung bas tshim par byas te/de dag gis bza' ba dang btung ba sngon ma myong ba thob nas bkres pa dang skom pa shin tu mi bzad pa chen po'i dbang du gyur pas ha cang mang du zos te/gsus pa rdol 
8b8(mdo sna tshogs): nas de dag gzhon nu bsod nams stobs la sems mngon par dga' bzhin du shi ste/dga' ldan gyi lha'i ris su skyes nas gzhon nu bsod nams stongs la gzhon nu khyod kyis bdag cag dga' ldan

9b1(mdo sna tshogs): 'bras bu 'grub par byed de/'di lta ste/zhing rmed pa rnams kyi 'bru dang /tshong pa rnams kyi khe dang /zham ring byed pa rnams kyi 'tsho ba dang /mi stod rims rnams kyi bstan bcos rtogs par bya ba dang /bsam 
9b2(mdo sna tshogs): gtan pa rnams kyi bsam gtan gyi 'bras bu thob par bya ba dang /de la sogs pa brtson 'grus kyi tshe 'di'i 'dod pa'i 'bras bu snang ngo //tshe phyi ma'i 'bras bu ni 'di lta ste/bde 'gro dang mtho ris kyi 
9b3(mdo sna tshogs): 'jig rten dang /longs spyod chen po dang /thar pa thob pa dang /de la sogs pa rtson 'grus kyi tshe phyi ma'i 'bras bu yang snang ngo //gzhan yang brtson 'grus ni yon tan thams cad kyi gzhir gyur pa dang /
9b4(mdo sna tshogs): gnod pa rnams zlog pa yin te/brtson 'grus la ni bsgrub dka' ba ci yang med do zhes brtson 'grus can gyis de skad ces smras pa la bzo dang ldan pas ba rgang de smras pa/khyod kyis bden zhing mang la rnam

9b6(mdo sna tshogs): phyir ro//de bas na bzo 'ba' shig mi rnams kyi nyams su dben pa yin no//gzhan yang bzo bo rigs dma' bar skyes su zin kyang rgyal po rnams dang /blon po rnams dang /bram ze dang /khyim bdag 
9b7(mdo sna tshogs): rnams kyis gdon mi za bar bkur ro//sna tshogs las can lha rnams dang //mi rnams kyis kyang lags par bkur//de bzhin ma rabs bzo yi phyir//mi dang lha rnams kyis kyang bkur//bzo dang ldan 
9b8(mdo sna tshogs): pas smras pa la shes rab can gyis yid tsam gyis brgad de smras pa/shes rab bzhin du mi rnams kyi nyams su byad gzugs kyang dben pa ma yin/brtson 'grus kyang dben pa ma yin no//bzo yang dben pa ma 
10a1(mdo sna tshogs): @#//yin no//de ci'i phyir zhe na/shes rab 'chal ba'i ni gzugs bzang ba mthong na yang rkyen du 'bab par 'dzem pa ma yin la/brtson 'grus kyis kyang don du skye bar mi 'gyur/bzo yang shes rab 
10a2(mdo sna tshogs): med na shes par mi nus pa'i phyir ro//de bas na shes rab 'ba' shig de ni thams cad bsgrub pa la mi rnams kyi nyams su dben pa yin no//gzhan yang shes rab kyis ni gzugs dang bzo//brtson 'grus nor yang 
10a3(mdo sna tshogs): thob par 'gyur//mi rnams legs dang bde ba che//shes rab kyis ni thams cad 'thob//ces shes rab can gyis de skad smras pa la gzhon nu bsod nams stobs kyis bzhin 'dzum dmul nas bsod nams dag gi 
10a4(mdo sna tshogs): stobs dngos su kha nas byung bzhin du smras pa/shes rab dang bral ba'i gzugs dang /bzo dang /brtson 'grus thams cad ni nyams su dben pa ma yin la/shes rab kyis yongs su zin pa ni 'bras bu dang 
10a5(mdo sna tshogs): bcas shing nyams su dben pa yin te/de ni bden pa kho na mod kyi 'on kyang shes rab de yang bsod nams med par thob par mi 'gyur te/de bas na bsod nams 'ba' shig mi rnams kyi nyams su dbain pa yin 
10a6(mdo sna tshogs): no//bsod nams shes bya ba ni gcig tu sdug pa'i 'bras bu rnams dang /phangs pa'i 'bras bu rnams dang dga' ba'i 'bras bu rnams dang yid du 'ong ba'i 'bras bu rnams dang /yid dang 'thad pa'i 
10a7(mdo sna tshogs): 'bras bu rnams kyi gtso bo yin te/yon tan ma lus par ngas brjod mi nus mod kyi/khyed khong du chud par bya ba'i phyir bsod nams dag gi yon tan phyogs gcig tsam bshad par byas nyon cig/bsod 
10a8(mdo sna tshogs): nams dag gis byad gzugs 'thob//bsod nams kyis ni tshul khrims ldan//bsod nams dag gis dpal 'thob cing //de bzhin longs spyod can du 'gyur//bsod nams dag gis shes rab 'thob//

10b2(mdo sna tshogs): skye ba dran//bsod nams dag gis grags pa 'thob//bsod nams dgag gis ngang tshul can//bsod nams dag gi gtod ldan 'gyur//bsod nams dag gis dbang po chod//bsod nams dag
10b3(mdo sna tshogs): gis bde ba 'thob//bsod nams dgagis mkhas pas mchod//bsod nams dag gisastobs 'thob cing //dam pa phrad pa bsod nams kyis//bsodanams dag gis don kun 'thob//

10b6(mdo sna tshogs): bsod nams dag gis bud med mchog/bsod nams chen pos nor dang 'bru//de yi 'dod dgu 'byung bar 'gyur//bsod nams dgag gis 'gro ba na//sa ni sdong dum tsher ma med//bsod nams
10b7(mdo sna tshogs): ji ltar ldan pa bzhin//mnyam gnas yang na gos kyang bting //ji ltar re ba tsam gyis na//nang bdag ni sos 'gyur bzhin//bsod nams dag gis reg gyur na//zas skom de bzhin gnyi ga dang //
10b8(mdo sna tshogs): gos dang rin chen 'bru dag kyang //rtag tu zad par mi 'gyur ro//lha klu lha min gnod sbyin dang //yi dgas lto 'phye mi rnams kyang //bsod nams bdag nyid che ba'i phyir//dug dang gnod pa rnams
11a1(mdo sna tshogs): las srung //bsod nams dag gis bsnyen bkur 'thob//skye bo mang dga' ba sod nams kyis//bsod nams dag gis grags ldan 'gyur//bsod nams dag gis skal bzang 'thob//
11a2(mdo sna tshogs): bsod nams dag gis tshig kyang brtsan//bsod nams dag gis sdug gur 'gyur//tshangs pa'i dbyangs 'thob bsod nams kyis//bsod nas kyis ni lus mdog sdug/bsod nams dag gis tshig
11a3(mdo sna tshogs): kyang bzang //drang ldan mdza' ldan gtso bor 'gyur//de dang der ni mkhas par 'ong //mi phyed pa yi mkhor dang ldan//bsod nams can ni nad med cing //smon pa'i gnas 'gyur bsod nams kyis//bsod
11a4(mdo sna tshogs): nams rnyed ldan gzi byin yod//bsod nams dag gis rgyal srid 'thob//'gran zla med cing gnod pa med//longs spyod mi nyams bsod nams kyis//bsodanams dag gis gter yang rnyed//'khor los
11a5(mdo sna tshogs): sgyur 'gyur bsod nams kyis/rin chen bdun ni mdun du 'gro//nam mkha'i pang nas kun tu rgyu//nyi zla dang yang gzi byin mnyam//zla ba nyid 'thob bsod nams kyis/bsod nams kyis 'thob nyi ma nyid//

11b1(mdo sna tshogs): gi phyogs 'dzin pas khas mi len to//de nas bsod nams stobs kyis de dag la yang smras pa/kye tshur sheg bdag cag shin tu ring pa gzhan gyi yul zhig tu dong ste/cha lugs mi mngon par 
11b2(mdo sna tshogs): 'khod na/'di ltar ci gzugs mi rnams kyi nyams su dben pa yin nam/'on te brtson 'grus sam/bzo 'am/shes rab 'am/bsod nams su dben pa shes par 'gyur ro//de nas gzhon nu 
11b3(mdo sna tshogs): de dag gis de ltar bya'o zhes byas nas gzhon nu bsod nams stobs kyi ltar mnyan te/yab la ma gsol bar tsham tshom med par shin tu ring pa rgyal khams gzhan dang /yul gzhan du dong nas rgyal po'i 
11b4(mdo sna tshogs): pho brang 'khor gzhan zhig tu zhugs te cha lugs mi mngon par 'khod de/de nas gzugs dang ldan pa ni mthong na yid du 'ong bas bsdus pa'i skye bo dag gis bshugs pa'i longs spyod tsam gyis 'tsho'o//

11b6(mdo sna tshogs): drangs nas 'tshong zhing de las byung ba'i longs spyod tsam gyis 'tshe'o//bzo dang ldan pa yang bzo ston pa las byung ba'i longs spyod cam gyis 'tsho'o//shes rab can gyis kyang tshod dpon gnyis kho na byung 
11b7(mdo sna tshogs): nas yun ring du lon pa shes rab kyi mkhas pas mdza' bar byas te/de nas de gnyis yongs su mgu bas nor byin pa tsam gyis bde bar 'tsho'o//gzhon nu bsod nams stobs kyang bdag gis bsod nams byas pa 
11b8(mdo sna tshogs): rnams kyi che ba nyid bsgrags pa'i phyir mi shin tu dbul po zhig gi khyim du zhugs nas/de gang gi nyin par khyim der zhugs pa de'i nyin par nyid du gzhon nu bsod nams stob kyi bsod nams kyi mthus khyim de 
12a1(mdo sna tshogs): @#//nyid kyi nang du 'byor pa chen po byung ste/khyim der nor dang /'bru dang /gser dngul mang pos yongs su gang bar gyur to//de nas mi dbul po de 'di ci zhig snyam du ya mtshan dang mchog tu dga' ba 
12a2(mdo sna tshogs): skyes te/sngon ma byung ba'i 'byor pa 'di ga las 'ongs snyam du sems so//de yang 'di snyam du khyim 'dir longs spyod 'byor pa byung ba 'di ni sngon ma mthong ba'i sems can 'di bdag gi khyim du 'ongs pa de'i mthu yin 
12a3(mdo sna tshogs): no snyam du sems so//de nas gzhon nu bsod nams stobs kyis longs spyod 'byor pa de dag mi dbul po de la ci bder spyod du bcug ste/de'i tshe mi dbul po de phyug cing nor mang la longs spyod che bar gyur to//
12a4(mdo sna tshogs): de nas 'di snyam du rtog ste/bdag sngon shin tu dbul zhing 'dug 'dug pa las/de bdag gi dpal dang skal pa bzang po 'byor bar gyur pa de dag thams cad ni gzhon nu 'di las brten te/gang gi phyir gang gi nyin mo 
12a5(mdo sna tshogs): gzhon nu 'di bdag gi khyim du 'odas pa de'i nyin par nyid du bdag gi khyim du dpal byung bar gyur pa las na/sems can 'di ni bsod nams kyis che bar grags pa zhig gis bkur sti bya ba'i rigs so snyam ste/de nas 
12a6(mdo sna tshogs): des de la rgyun mi 'chad par bkur sti shin tu chen pos bkur sti byas nas mi dbul po che ge mo zhig gi khyim du gzhon nu sngon ma byung ba zhig 'ongs te/de'i mthus khyim der dpal byung bar gyur to zhes kun tu sgra

12b1(mdo sna tshogs): de nas skye bo mang po bsod nams dag gis sems dga' bar byas te/ltad mo lta 'dod par gyur nas de lta ba'i phyir dong ngo//de nas gzhon nu bsod nams stobs kyis skye bo lhags pa de dag bsdu bar 
12b2(mdo sna tshogs): 'dod de/kye ma bdag la khyim rin po che thams cad kyis gang zhing 'tshog chas rnam pa sna tshogs dang ldan la bran dang bran mo dang las byed pas gang ba rnam pa sna tshogs blta na yid du 'ong ba zhig byung na ci ma 
12b3(mdo sna tshogs): rung snyam du sems bskyed do//dge slong de dag sems bskyed ma thag tu gzhon nu bsod nams stongs kyi khyim chen po rin po che thams cad kyi gang zhing 'tshog chas rnams pa sna tshogs dang ldan la/bran dang 
12b4(mdo sna tshogs): bran mo dang las byed pas gong ba rnam pa sna tshogs lta ba yid du 'ong ba glo bur du byung ngo//de mthong nas kyang skye bo mang po dag mchog tu ngo mtshar du gyur te/bsod nams dag la gces su 'dzin pa yang dag par 
12b5(mdo sna tshogs): skyes nas/kye ma bsod nams dag gis 'bras bu ni dang ra ro zhes rtogs nas gzhon nu bsod nams stobs la bkur sti chen pos bkur sti byas so//gzhon nu bsod nams stob kyis kyang skye bo mang po de dag

25b1(mdo sna tshogs): ldan par gyur to//dge slong dag de'i tshe de'i dus na 'dzam bu'i gling gi mi rnams kyi tshe'i tshad lo bzhi khri bzhi stong par gyur to//dge slong ngag rgyal po zla 'od ni byang chub sems dpar gyur te/thams cad ster ba/thams cad yongs 
25b2(mdo sna tshogs): su gtong ba ste/des rgyal po'i pho brang rdo bzangs nas phyung ste/grong khyer gyi shar phyogs su gdugs dang rgyal mtshan dang ba dan sgreng ste/gser gyi rnga po che yang dag par brdungs nas sbyin pa ni gtong /bsod nams ni byed do//

25b4(mdo sna tshogs): rnams la ni skom/bza' ba dang bca' ba dang /phreng ba dang mar me dang /dri dang byug pa dang mal cha dang stan dang rgyab rten dang /skye bo rnams kyi gnas dang /gdugs dang shing rta dang spud pa dang /rgyan dang gser gyi snod dngul gyi phye 
25b5(mdo sna tshogs): mas yongs su gang ba rnams dang /dngul gyi snod gser gyi phye mas yongs su gang ba rnams/gser gyi rwa btags pa'i ba lang rnams dang /khar ba'i bzho snod rnams dang /gzhon nu ma rgyan thams cad kyis brgyan par byas

29b5(mdo sna tshogs): sgor song ba dang /grong khyer gyi lha mo de bzlog ste/gang 'di lha mo shes par gyis shig/lha bram ze 'di 'gugs kyis bram ze 'di thong cig/de nas grong khyer na gnas pa'i lha mos zla ba chen po la 'di skad ces smras so//
29b6(mdo sna tshogs): gang yang zla ba chen po shes par gyis shig/bram ze 'di ni gtum pa'i sems dang /snying rje med pa'i sems dang /mi zad pa'i sems kyis rgyal po zla 'od mgong brog pa'i phyir rgyal po'i pho brang rdo bzangs su 'ongs na/khyed la
